We are excited to announce the 9th Convocation 2025, which will be held on Saturday 1st February 2025 from 10:00 AM to 8:30 PM, and we would like you to be a part of this significant event as a volunteer!
As a volunteer, you will play a key role in ensuring the success of the ceremony and contribute to creating an unforgettable experience for the graduates, their families, and all attendees. 

We are looking for enthusiastic, responsible students who are willing to dedicate their time and energy to help with various tasks throughout the Day.

Event Details:

Date: Saturday 1st February, 2025
Time: 10:00 AM to 8:30 PM
Location: Ajeenkya DY Patil University, Lohegaon, Pune
Volunteer Responsibilities:

Assisting with guest registration
Guiding attendees to their seats
Supporting the smooth flow of the ceremony
Helping with stage setups and other logistical tasks
If you are interested in volunteering, please sign up by filling this form. Volunteering at this event will not only allow you to be part of this important occasion but also provide valuable experience in event management.

Thank you for considering this opportunity, and we look forward to having you as part of the team!

[05/02/25, 4:45:31 PM] Pratham Sharma Adypu: DAY 1: PUNE Assemble at the railway station and board trains to Cochin, Overnight Journey
DAY 2: COCHIN
Upon arriving in the morning at Ernakulam Junction (Cochin), board the bus to the hotel. Cochin is one of the largest ports of India, a major naval base, and one of the fi nest natural harbors in the world. Check-in at the hotel, Fresh-n-up & Lunch. Post Lunch visit Mattancherry palace, Enjoy Sunset at the Chinese fi shing net or Kalari/Kathakali Show (Cost on Own). Evening visit Marine Drive and the local market for shopping. Late evening back to the hotel for dinner. Dinner and overnight stay at the hotel.
(Meals: L-D)
DAY 3: COCHIN-MUNNAR
Morning after breakfast, drive to Munnar. Enroute lunch and visit Athirapally Waterfall. The tea gardens and cardamom plantations give Munnar a beautiful look. Evening Check-in & Fresh-up.
DAY 4: MUNNAR
Have a morning breakfast and then proceed to Mattupetty Dam. It has the highest peak, Anamudi, in South India and is home to animals like elephants and the rare Nilgiri Tahrs. Enroute visit the Tea Museum & Echo point. Evening back to the hotel. After dinner, Overnight stay at a hotel.
(Meals: B-L-D)
DAY 5: MUNNAR-ALLEPPEY (5 to 6 Hours Journey)
After breakfast, Check-out and drive to Alleppey for a backwater houseboat. Alleppey is undoubtedly the most invigorating and fascinating paradise in Kerala. This charming emerald peninsula is situated in the heart of the backwaters. Leisure till dinner. Dinner and overnight stay at a houseboat.
(Meals: B-L-D)
DAY 6: ALLEPPEY-COCHIN
Morning Early Breakfast proceeds to Ernakulam Junction to board the Train for your return journey to Mumbai.
(Meals: B)
DAY 7: HOMETOWN
Arrive home with cherished memories and a wealth of industrial knowledge,
preserving the happiness of these moments for a lifetime.

[17/02/25, 9:18:37 AM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: ### **Practical Title:**  
**Census Survey of 10 Families: Data Collection and Analysis Using Sampling Methods**  

---

### **AIM:**  
To conduct a census survey of 10 families and analyze the collected data using appropriate sampling methods to understand demographic, economic, and social patterns.  

---

### **OBJECTIVES:**  
1. To understand the process of conducting a census survey.  
2. To apply various sampling methods for data collection.  
3. To analyze demographic and economic data of the selected families.  
4. To interpret and visualize data using statistical tools.  
5. To draw meaningful conclusions about household characteristics based on survey results.  

---

### **PROCEDURE:**  
1. **Selection of Families:**  
   - Choose 10 families randomly or using a specific sampling technique (e.g., Simple Random Sampling, Systematic Sampling, or Stratified Sampling).  

2. **Designing the Questionnaire:**  
   - Prepare a structured questionnaire covering key aspects such as:  
     - Family size  
     - Age distribution  
     - Educational background  
     - Employment status  
     - Income level  
     - Living conditions  

3. **Data Collection:**  
   - Conduct surveys through personal interviews, phone calls, or online forms.  
   - Record responses accurately and systematically.  

4. **Data Organization & Preprocessing:**  
   - Tabulate the collected data in a structured format (e.g., Excel, Google Sheets, or statistical tools).  
   - Clean and validate the data to remove inconsistencies.  

5. **Data Analysis:**  
   - Use statistical techniques such as mean, median, mode, frequency distribution, and percentage analysis.  
   - Apply sampling methods to analyze trends and correlations.  
   - Represent findings using graphs, charts, and tables.  

6. **Interpretation & Discussion:**  
   - Compare the results with existing demographic data.  
   - Identify key insights such as income disparities, literacy rates, and employment trends.  

---

### **CONCLUSION:**  
The census survey of 10 families provided valuable insights into demographic and economic patterns within a small population sample. By applying sampling methods and statistical analysis, we gained an understanding of household characteristics, social conditions, and economic variations. This practical demonstrated the importance of data collection and interpretation in real-world applications, highlighting the role of sampling techniques in decision-making and policy formulation.

[17/02/25, 10:04:57 AM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: ### **Practical Title:**  
**"Analyzing Top 100 Ranked Universities: A Case Study of One University Using Sampling Methods and Sampling Proportions"**  

---

### **AIM:**  
To study and analyze one university from the top 100 ranked universities using appropriate sampling methods and sampling proportions to evaluate various academic, infrastructure, and research parameters.  

---

### **OBJECTIVES:**  
1. To understand different sampling methods for selecting one university from the top 100 ranked universities.  
2. To apply appropriate sampling proportions in data analysis.  
3. To analyze key parameters such as academic reputation, research output, faculty-student ratio, and infrastructure.  
4. To interpret and present data using statistical and graphical techniques.  
5. To derive meaningful insights about the university's ranking factors and performance.  

---

### **PROCEDURE:**  

1. **Selection of Sampling Method:**  
   - Choose a suitable sampling technique to select one university from the list of top 100 ranked universities.  
   - Possible methods include:  
     - **Simple Random Sampling (SRS):** Selecting a university randomly.  
     - **Systematic Sampling:** Selecting every k-th university from a ranked list.  
     - **Stratified Sampling:** Dividing universities into different categories (e.g., based on country or type) and selecting one from a specific category.  

2. **Defining Sampling Proportions:**  
   - Determine the proportion of data to be analyzed (e.g., faculty-student ratio, research output, international collaboration).  
   - Use proportional sampling to ensure fair representation of key indicators.  

3. **Data Collection:**  
   - Gather university-related data from official websites, ranking reports, or research publications.  
   - Focus on metrics such as:  
     - Academic excellence (faculty quality, student performance)  
     - Research publications and citations  
     - Infrastructure and campus facilities  
     - Student diversity and international rankings  

4. **Data Analysis & Visualization:**  
   - Organize collected data in a structured format (e.g., tables, graphs, charts).  
   - Apply statistical techniques to analyze trends and proportions.  
   - Compare the selected university's metrics with overall trends in top-ranked universities.  

5. **Interpretation & Discussion:**  
   - Identify factors contributing to the university’s ranking.  
   - Discuss strengths and areas for improvement based on comparative analysis.  

---

### **CONCLUSION:**  
Through this study, we successfully analyzed a single university from the top 100 ranked universities using systematic sampling methods and proportional data analysis. The findings provided insights into academic performance, research excellence, and institutional strengths. This practical demonstrated the importance of sampling techniques in data-driven decision-making and higher education analytics.

[17/02/25, 10:13:17 AM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: Practical Title:
"Analyzing Top 100 Global Companies: A Detailed Case Study of One Company Using Simple Random Sampling Method"

AIM:
To conduct a detailed analysis of one company from the top 100 global companies using the Simple Random Sampling (SRS) method and evaluate its financial performance, market position, innovation strategies, and overall business operations.

OBJECTIVES:
To understand the application of the Simple Random Sampling (SRS) method in selecting a company from the top 100 global companies.
To study the key business and financial parameters of the selected company.
To analyze the company's market position, revenue trends, and innovation strategies.
To interpret and present findings using statistical and graphical methods.
To draw meaningful insights into the company's success factors and global influence.
PROCEDURE:
Selection of Sampling Method:

Use the Simple Random Sampling (SRS) method to select one company from the list of the top 100 global companies.
Random selection can be done using:
Lottery Method (Assigning numbers to companies and randomly selecting one).
Computer-Generated Random Selection (Using software like Python, Excel, or an online randomizer).
Data Collection:

Gather data on the selected company from reliable sources like:
Company’s official reports and financial statements.
Stock market data and annual revenue reports.
Business journals, market research reports, and news articles.
Analysis of Key Metrics:

Study the company’s financial performance, including:
Revenue, net profit, and market capitalization.
Growth trends over the last five years.
Examine business strategies:
Market expansion, innovation, and technology adoption.
Competitive advantage and industry ranking.
Evaluate global impact:
Presence in international markets.
Contribution to employment and sustainability initiatives.
Data Interpretation & Visualization:

Organize findings in tables, charts, and graphs for better understanding.
Compare the selected company's performance with industry trends.
Discussion & Insights:

Identify factors contributing to the company's global ranking.
Discuss the company’s strengths, weaknesses, opportunities, and threats (SWOT Analysis).
CONCLUSION:
Using the Simple Random Sampling method, we successfully selected and analyzed a company from the top 100 global companies. The detailed study provided valuable insights into its financial performance, business strategies, and global market position. This practical demonstrated the effectiveness of random sampling in business analysis and emphasized the importance of data-driven decision-making in corporate research.

[08/03/25, 1:24:37 PM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: Practical Title:
"Analyzing Top 100 Global Companies: A Detailed Case Study of One Company Using Simple Random Sampling Method"

AIM:
To conduct a detailed analysis of one company from the top 100 global companies using the Simple Random Sampling (SRS) method and evaluate its financial performance, market position, innovation strategies, and overall business operations.

OBJECTIVES:
To understand the application of the Simple Random Sampling (SRS) method in selecting a company from the top 100 global companies.
To study the key business and financial parameters of the selected company.
To analyze the company's market position, revenue trends, and innovation strategies.
To interpret and present findings using statistical and graphical methods.
To draw meaningful insights into the company's success factors and global influence.
PROCEDURE:
Selection of Sampling Method:

Use the Simple Random Sampling (SRS) method to select one company from the list of the top 100 global companies.
Random selection can be done using:
Lottery Method (Assigning numbers to companies and randomly selecting one).
Computer-Generated Random Selection (Using software like Python, Excel, or an online randomizer).
Data Collection:

Gather data on the selected company from reliable sources like:
Company’s official reports and financial statements.
Stock market data and annual revenue reports.
Business journals, market research reports, and news articles.
Analysis of Key Metrics:

Study the company’s financial performance, including:
Revenue, net profit, and market capitalization.
Growth trends over the last five years.
Examine business strategies:
Market expansion, innovation, and technology adoption.
Competitive advantage and industry ranking.
Evaluate global impact:
Presence in international markets.
Contribution to employment and sustainability initiatives.
Data Interpretation & Visualization:

Organize findings in tables, charts, and graphs for better understanding.
Compare the selected company's performance with industry trends.
Discussion & Insights:

Identify factors contributing to the company's global ranking.
Discuss the company’s strengths, weaknesses, opportunities, and threats (SWOT Analysis).
CONCLUSION:
Using the Simple Random Sampling method, we successfully selected and analyzed a company from the top 100 global companies. The detailed study provided valuable insights into its financial performance, business strategies, and global market position. This practical demonstrated the effectiveness of random sampling in business analysis and emphasized the importance of data-driven decision-making in corporate research.

[28/03/25, 9:12:32 AM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: ### **Practical: Survey on Favorite Snack Among Students**  

#### **Objective:**  
To determine the proportion and percentage of students who prefer different types of snacks.  

#### **Materials Needed:**  
- A survey sheet or Google Form  
- Pen and paper (if done manually)  
- Calculator or spreadsheet software  

#### **Procedure:**  
1. **Select a Sample:** Choose a random sample of 50 students from a school or college.  
2. **Collect Data:** Ask each student their favorite snack from the following categories:  
   - Chips  
   - Chocolate  
   - Fruits  
   - Biscuits  
3. **Record Responses:** Tally the number of students who chose each snack.  
4. **Calculate Proportions:**  
   - Use the formula:  
     \[
     \text{Proportion} = \frac{\text{Number of students choosing a snack}}{\text{Total students surveyed}}
     \]  
   - Example: If 20 students prefer chips, the proportion is  
     \[
     \frac{20}{50} = 0.4
     \]  
5. **Convert to Percentage:**  
   - Multiply the proportion by 100:  
     \[
     0.4 \times 100 = 40\%
     \]  
6. **Analyze and Interpret:**  
   - Compare the proportions and percentages for each snack.  
   - Identify the most and least preferred snack.  

#### **Expected Outcome:**  
A clear understanding of how sampling proportions and percentages are used to analyze survey data in real-world scenarios.  

Would you like a variation of this practical for a different context? 😊

[02/04/25, 9:32:09 PM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: Practical 5: Stratified Random Sampling in Population Surveys
Title:
Application of Stratified Random Sampling in Estimating Population Characteristics

Objective:
To demonstrate how stratified random sampling improves the precision of population estimates.

To compare stratified random sampling with simple random sampling in terms of efficiency.

Materials Needed:
Population dataset (e.g., students' test scores, household income data, etc.)

Calculator or statistical software (Excel, SPSS, R, Python)

Random number generator or sampling table

Procedure:
Define the population and identify stratification criteria (e.g., gender, age group, income level).

Divide the population into mutually exclusive strata.

Determine the sample size for each stratum using proportional allocation or Neyman allocation.

Select random samples from each stratum using a random number generator.

Calculate the sample mean and variance for each stratum.

Compare the precision of the estimates with those obtained using simple random sampling.

Data Collection:
Record data for each sampled unit within each stratum.

Organize the data based on stratification criteria.

Compute the population mean and variance using both stratified and simple random sampling.

Conclusion:
Stratified random sampling provides more precise estimates than simple random sampling when the population is heterogeneous.

The efficiency of stratified sampling depends on the correct identification of strata and the chosen allocation method.
[02/04/25, 9:32:10 PM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: Practical 6: Estimating Population Characteristics Using Equal Cluster Sampling
Title:
Analysis of Efficiency in Equal Cluster Sampling

Objective:
To understand the concept of equal cluster sampling and evaluate its efficiency.

To examine the relationship between intra-class correlation and cluster sampling efficiency.

Materials Needed:
Population dataset (e.g., student test scores grouped by schools, households within districts, etc.)

Calculator or statistical software (Excel, SPSS, R, Python)

Random number generator or sampling table

Procedure:
Define the Population and Identify Clusters:

Group the population into equal-sized clusters (e.g., schools, districts, or neighborhoods).

Select a Sample of Clusters:

Use random sampling to select a predefined number of clusters from the population.

Estimate Population Parameters:

Compute the sample mean and variance for each selected cluster.

Estimate the intra-class correlation coefficient (ρ) to determine how similar observations are within clusters.

Calculate Efficiency of Cluster Sampling:

Compare cluster sampling variance with that of simple random sampling (SRS) to assess efficiency.

Data Collection:
Record data from all individuals within selected clusters.

Compute cluster means and variances.

Compare the efficiency of cluster sampling with that of SRS.

Conclusion:
Cluster sampling is efficient when intra-class correlation is low (i.e., clusters are internally diverse).

When clusters are too homogeneous, variance increases, reducing efficiency compared to SRS.

Equal cluster sampling is useful for logistical simplicity and cost reduction.

[04/04/25, 11:55:19 AM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: Practical No 7- Practical Applications of Sampling Methods in Data Science for IT
Sampling techniques are widely used in IT and data science for tasks such as machine learning model training, A/B testing, cybersecurity analysis, system performance evaluation, and customer segmentation. Below is a detailed explanation of how each sampling method is applied in IT, with practical examples.
________________________________________
1. Simple Random Sampling in IT
Application:
Used in machine learning model training, A/B testing, and customer feedback analysis to ensure an unbiased representation of the dataset.
Example:
A social media platform wants to analyze user engagement. The company has a database of 1 million users and wants to sample 10,000 users randomly for sentiment analysis.
•	A random number generator selects these 10,000 users from the database.
•	This unbiased sample is used to train a machine learning model for predicting engagement patterns.
Use Cases in IT:
✔ Training AI models with unbiased datasets.
✔ Performing A/B testing for a website redesign.
✔ Extracting user feedback from app reviews.
________________________________________
2. Stratified Sampling in IT
Application:
Used when dealing with imbalanced datasets, ensuring fair representation of different categories (e.g., device types, user demographics, fraud detection).
Example:
An e-commerce company wants to analyze customer buying behavior based on three income levels:
•	Low-income (30%)
•	Middle-income (50%)
•	High-income (20%)
Since the high-income group is smaller, simple random sampling might underrepresent it. Instead, stratified sampling ensures proportional representation from each group.
Use Cases in IT:
✔ Preventing bias in classification algorithms (e.g., fraud detection).
✔ Ensuring fair representation in user testing across different age groups.
✔ Balancing training data in machine learning models dealing with diverse populations.
________________________________________
3. Systematic Sampling in IT
Application:
Used for log monitoring, system performance testing, and periodic data extraction.
Example:
A cloud service provider wants to monitor server logs for anomalies. Instead of analyzing terabytes of log data, they use systematic sampling by selecting every 1,000th log entry.
•	If total logs = 1 billion, they extract 1 million logs systematically.
•	This helps in identifying unusual spikes in CPU usage, network traffic, or security breaches.
Use Cases in IT:
✔ Efficient log sampling for cybersecurity anomaly detection.
✔ Performance testing by selecting every nth request in a web server.
✔ Quality assurance in software testing by checking every 50th test case.
________________________________________
4. Cluster Sampling in IT
Application:
Used for geographically distributed data centers, cloud performance analysis, and regional user behavior studies.
Example:
A global streaming platform (e.g., Netflix, YouTube) wants to analyze video buffering issues.
•	Instead of randomly sampling individual users worldwide, they randomly select 5 entire countries (clusters) and analyze all users within those clusters.
This reduces cost while still providing valuable insights into regional performance issues.
Use Cases in IT:
✔ Testing cloud infrastructure in randomly selected data center regions.
✔ Studying regional cybersecurity threats by analyzing specific locations.
✔ Conducting user behavior analysis in selected cities instead of a worldwide survey.

1. Implement High Availability and Fault Tolerance
2. use multiple Availability Zones (AZs)
3. Regions to ensure redundancy,
 4. application across multiple instances or containers to handle
 failures without downtime
5. IAM controls to ensure proper user access and
 permissions
6. Encrypt sensitive data at rest and in transit
7. Automate Regular updates and patches to the software stack to mitigate
 security vulnerabilities
8. detect and respond to security events.
9. Regularly Test and Validate Resilience
10. test backup 
11. restore data integrity and availability
12. Protect against data loss due to disasters or system failures.

[05/05/25, 4:36:38 PM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: Data mining 5th assignment 

1. What is text mining? Discuss its importance, core concepts, and provide examples of its practical applications.

2. What are the key processes involved in text mining, and how do they contribute to extracting meaningful insights from unstructured text?

3. Explain the architecture of a typical text mining system. What are its major components and their roles in processing text data?

4. How does document representation impact the performance of text categorization? Why is it important in the context of text mining?

5. What role does machine learning play in text mining? How does it support tasks such as classification, clustering, and pattern recognition?
6. How can visualization and analytical techniques such as word clouds, keyword frequency analysis, and clustering be used to analyze and organize textual data? Illustrate with examples like customer feedback analysis.

[01/11/25, 10:14:12 PM] ~ Devesh Pandey: Dear All,
We are pleased to announce Sustainability Week 2025 – “Living the Change”, organized by the ADYPU Green Ambassadors & Media Team, supported by Sukhvinder Multani – Research & Publication, ADYPU.


The celebration will be held from 3rd to 6th November 2025, featuring a series of engaging and eco-conscious activities.

Day 1 – 3rd November
Sustainable Greetings – “Gifting with a Purpose”


All the students, teaching and non teaching staff can exchange gifts with each other items such as  -  handmade cards, potted plants, recycled crafts, or reusable items etc promoting mindful gifting and waste-free celebrations.
Venue: ULC 1 | Time: 3:30 PM – 4:30 PM



Day 2 – 4th November
Online Poster Making – “The Green Canvas”
Students create posters on sustainability and SDGs using recycled or digital tools.
Time: 11:00 AM – 3:00 PM



Day 3 – 5th November
Green Shade Attire – “Dress for the Planet”
Dress in sustainable or green-themed attire such as khadi, cotton, handloom, or upcycled fabrics to promote eco-friendly fashion.



Day 4 – 6th November
Cinema on Sustainability – “Reel to Real”
Film screening on climate change and sustainable living followed by a short reflection session.
Venue: ULC Mini Auditorium, 7th Floor | Time: 3:30 PM – 4:30 PM

Students can fill the form below and upload their  completed posters for the Poster  competition. The entries will close at 3.00pm.

https://forms.gle/35Auu3eXMAFYe2hP6

Let’s come together to celebrate sustainability and make a positive difference on our campus!

Warm regards,
ADYPU Green Ambassadors & Media Team
Supported by Sukhvinder Multani – Research & Publication, ADYPU

[25/11/25, 6:01:48 AM] Gopal Tanwar Adypu Cr: About the Role

You will own the technical stability, performance, and continuous improvement of the Sky Buddy mobile app (Android + iOS). Your job is straightforward: keep the app running flawlessly, fix issues fast, and roll out improvements without excuses.


Key Responsibilities

1. App Maintenance

Monitor app health, crashes, and performance (Firebase, Play Console, Xcode logs).

Fix bugs, UI glitches, backend errors, and API failures quickly.

Ensure API endpoints stay live, optimized, and secure.

Maintain app compatibility with the latest Android/iOS versions.

Keep dependencies, SDKs, and packages updated.


2. Frontend (Mobile App)

Implement UI/UX changes based on Sky Buddy guidelines.

Improve loading speed, responsiveness, and animations.

Optimize screens, navigation, and user interactions.

Ensure all features work seamlessly across devices.

NOTE:- Sky Buddy uses React Native


3. Backend & APIs

Maintain and optimize backend architecture (Node.js / Firebase / Supabase / PHP — adapt to your stack).
NOTE :- Sky Buddy runs on Node.js

Enhance API efficiency, security, and uptime.

Manage databases, authentication, and server logic.

Perform regular backups, security checks, and version upgrades.


4. Feature Enhancements

Add new modules and improve existing ones (Find a Buddy, Be a Buddy, Travel with a Buddy, Requests, etc.).

Integrate 3rd-party APIs where needed.

Deliver new features with clean, well-documented code.

NOTE - Not necessary currently, but may require in future.

5. Deployment & Store Management

Handle signed APK/IPA builds and store submissions.

Manage updates for Galaxy Store and App Store.

Fix review rejections fast and ensure compliance with guidelines.


Must-Have Skills

Strong experience in Flutter / React Native (pick whichever stack Sky Buddy currently uses).

Strong backend experience in Node.js, Firebase, Supabase, or Express.

Knowledge of relational & non-relational databases (MySQL / MongoDB / Firestore).

API design + integration.

Version control (GitHub).

Debugging mindset — able to fix issues fast without hand-holding.


Nice-to-Have

Experience with real-time chat, push notifications, and location-based features.

UI/UX understanding.

Experience in scaling consumer apps.

Basic cloud knowledge (AWS / Google Cloud).


Working Style & Expectations

Fast response time for urgent issues. (Will adjust UK time zone)

Clear communication no vague explanations.

Deliver stable builds without breaking existing features.

Weekly reporting on:

Bugs fixed

Updates made

Issues pending

New improvements

Stipend - 5000/- per month
